| 0.3 | SAY 1/5 | 23_stories:opening | You are Qranchod, copyist of Hosisiaq, Arago (Arachnid) by birth and of the Witnesses of Haqsh'zes by faith. You have read of Sunsirere since you were a child. |
| 0.3 | SAY 2/5 | 23_stories:opening | You stand in Ma'kan'ji, in dry season, by day. The quest is yours to lead, with Culzan's backing. |
| 0.3 | SAY 3/5 | 23_stories:opening | The clock: 7 stops, each with a par time; the appointed time at Sunsirere is par Day 400, 06:22. There, every day early counts for you; late costs you . |
| 0.3 | SAY 4/5 | 23_stories:opening | In Hosisiaq, ask the innkeeper about who would join the company. |
| 0.3 | SAY 5/5 | 23_stories:opening | But first: Meet the company in Ma'kan'ji. |

| 0.3 | SAY 3/6 | 06_game:town | “People speak of a fair somewhere east, 1,500 miles off or so, in Srajajas.” |
| 0.3 | SAY 4/6 | 06_game:town | Mor'than, for one, will hardly look at you: the Aj'Snaga (Serpents) here think little of yours; you keep another faith than the Xult'jism . |
| 0.3 | SAY 5/6 | 06_game:town | For the road to Hosisiaq (6,115 mi by sailing boat), Mor'than reckons 98 days: 98 rations for you. You carry 13 . |
| 0.3 | SAY 6/6 | 06_game:town | A room costs a stranger's price here. |

| 188.4 | SAY 5/6 | 06_game:town | For the road to Unzinb (2,045 mi by sailing ship), Srash reckons 10 days: 10 rations for you. You carry 0 . |
| 188.4 | SAY 6/6 | 06_game:town | A room costs a stranger's price here. |

| 229.8 | SAY 1/6 | 06_game:town | Bulunzadd keeps the inn , and takes you in: a Arago (Arachnid) stranger. |
| 229.8 | SAY 2/6 | 06_game:town | On the table: preserved food, vinegar, cheese, all made here . |
| 229.8 | SAY 3/6 | 06_game:town | “People speak of a ruin somewhere north, 1,250 miles off or so, in Kepja.” |
| 229.8 | SAY 4/6 | 06_game:town | Bulunzadd, for one, deals with you coolly: the Khazadur (Dwarven) here think little of yours; you keep another faith than the Shundunism . |
| 229.8 | SAY 5/6 | 06_game:town | For the road to Rhecad (5,807 mi by rowboat), Bulunzadd reckons 209 days: 209 rations for you. You carry 20 . |
| 229.8 | SAY 6/6 | 06_game:town | A room costs a stranger's price here. |
| 229.8 | CHOOSE | 06_game:town | Unzinb · Day 230, 18:44 · 🟡 1.48 · food 20 rations ⟶ [★ Listen for rumours] [Harbour and coach office: sailings, coaches, boats for hire] [Market: food 🟡 0.04 a day; goods to buy and sell] [Inn: a room until morning (🟡 0.32)] [Give alms at the shrine (🟡 0.64)] [Notice board: work and errands] [Harbourmaster: Kizad] [Innkeeper: Bulunzadd] [Merchant: Nuzunbahag] [Shrine-keeper: Kholukkharb] [Cap |
| 229.8 | SAY 1/3 | 06_game:town | Kholukkharb keeps the shrine of the Shundunism: Organized, Monotheism; they pray to Shundun, The Prime Reaper . |
| 229.8 | SAY 2/3 | 06_game:town | You are not of this faith. You are given water and courtesy, no more . |
| 229.8 | SAY 3/3 | 06_game:town | Kholukkharb, too, will hardly look at you. |
| 229.8 | CHOOSE | 06_game:town | Unzinb · Day 230, 18:44 · 🟡 1.48 · food 20 rations ⟶ [★ Listen for rumours] [Harbour and coach office: sailings, coaches, boats for hire] [Market: food 🟡 0.04 a day; goods to buy and sell] [Inn: a room until morning (🟡 0.32)] [Give alms at the shrine (🟡 0.64)] [Notice board: work and errands] [Harbourmaster: Kizad] [Innkeeper: Bulunzadd] [Merchant: Nuzunbahag] [Shrine-keeper: Kholukkharb] [Cap |
| 229.8 | SAY 1/5 | 06_game:town | Zizaddum speaks for United Provinces of Bilamunz, a union . Your people stand to theirs as: Friendly . |
| 229.8 | SAY 2/5 | 06_game:town | Allies: Albeyru, Nucoz, Krazur . |
| 229.8 | SAY 3/5 | 06_game:town | Taxes: 8% on sales, 16% poll . |
| 229.8 | SAY 4/5 | 06_game:town | Zizaddum, too, deals with you coolly. |
| 229.8 | SAY 5/5 | 06_game:town | The watch here protects the crown's subjects; strangers are at its pleasure. |

| 229.8 | SAY 1/4 | 06_game:town | Unzinb: a city of 23,907 Khazadur (Dwarven) people (Naval) , with walls, plaza, port. |
| 229.8 | SAY 2/4 | 06_game:town | Someone says: There are stories of a dungeon far to the north, past where anyone here has been. Bigger with every telling. |
| 229.8 | SAY 3/4 | 06_game:town | Khulukkhur, too, deals with you coolly. |
| 229.8 | SAY 4/4 | 06_game:town | About what the company has not been told: they are sure of it, and point you on to Rhecad . |

| 281.6 | MODAL | 11_screens:journal | Journal The traveller Qranchod, 41, copyist (Stranger from Hosisiaq; well-connected: news reaches you faster, and errands pay a fifth more). You keep the Witnesses of Haqsh'zes of Hosisiaq, and you travel to Sunsirere on an errand you have told no one. Arago (Arachnid); faith: Witnesses of Haqsh'zes. Carries a small icon of Haqsh'zes. Purse, stores, condition, standing🟡 0.1 · food 0 rations · goo |
| 281.6 | MODAL | ?:? | The journey You have read of Sunsirere since you were a child. | 383.4 | SAY 1/7 | 06_game:town | Eldergaven keeps the inn , and takes you in: a Arago (Arachnid) stranger. |
| 383.4 | SAY 2/7 | 06_game:town | On the table: preserved food, all made here . |
| 383.4 | SAY 3/7 | 06_game:town | “There are stories of a dungeon far to the south-west, past where anyone here has been. Bigger with every telling.” |
| 383.4 | SAY 4/7 | 06_game:town | Eldergaven, for one, will hardly look at you: the Dail (Human) here think little of yours; you keep another faith than the Simehalerism . |
| 383.4 | SAY 5/7 | 06_game:town | For the road to Caven (633 mi by on foot (light)), Eldergaven reckons 19 days: 19 rations for you. You carry 4 . |
| 383.4 | SAY 6/7 | 06_game:town | A room costs a stranger's price here. |
| 383.4 | SAY 7/7 | 06_game:town | About a bed and the news: they tell you what you came to learn, and point you on to Caven . |

| 383.4 | SAY 1/4 | 06_game:town | Hearthall keeps the shrine of the Simehalerism: Organized, Monotheism; they pray to Simehalere, The Patient Hippogriff of Love . |
| 383.4 | SAY 2/4 | 06_game:town | You are not of this faith, but your road ends in Sunsirere, and that is enough to be welcome . |
| 383.4 | SAY 3/4 | 06_game:town | The faith's seat is Cryshield, 1,010 mi south . |
| 383.4 | SAY 4/4 | 06_game:town | Hearthall, too, will hardly look at you. |

F follows the plan's gold line; with a waypoint it takes the shortest water. Boats keep near the coast, ships keep off it. A boat's hands stop at dusk; a ship with hands for two watches sails on. In a storm, small hulls want a beach and ships want open water. ⟶ [Thank him] |

| 428.4 | SAY 3/6 | 06_game:town | “A sickness, far to the south-west, past where anyone here has been.” |

| 428.4 | SAY 5/6 | 06_game:town | For the road to Sunsirere (2,980 mi by ship for the crossing, with a sea crossing the plan does not show), Little reckons 15 days: 15 rations for you. You carry 19 . |

| 463.7 | MODAL | 11_screens:journal | Journal The traveller Qranchod, 41, copyist (Stranger from Hosisiaq; well-connected: news reaches you faster, and errands pay a fifth more). You keep the Witnesses of Haqsh'zes of Hosisiaq, and you travel to Sunsirere on an errand you have told no one. Arago (Arachnid); faith: Witnesses of Haqsh'zes. Carries a small icon of Haqsh'zes. Purse, stores, condition, standing🟡 0.02 · food 4 rations · go |
| 463.7 | MODAL | ?:? | The journey You have read of Sunsirere since you were a child. You lead. ◇ Reach Sunsirere with the company whole: a long crossing of temperate rainforest; the way there crosses the sea, so look for a passage.
